show the prompt every model received 
I need an amazing, impressive, extraordinary website for **WastedToken Mint**, a


company that mints collectible physical challenge coins.





Deliver **one self-contained `app.html`**. No external dependencies, no network


calls, no external image or asset URLs — every visual is procedural or inline.


Complete the entire project in one pass, no questions.





## Hero — the coin





A single large challenge coin, rendered and animated entirely by you. WebGL,


Three.js (inlined), or 2D canvas — your choice. The coin must:





- **Rotate in 3D about the vertical axis AND flip about the horizontal axis**, so


  both faces are seen during one loop.


- Show a **front face** embossed with the WastedToken Mint emblem in raised


  relief, and a **back face** showing a denomination as a token count (e.g.


  "10,000 TOKENS"). The two faces must look clearly different.


- Have a **reeded (ridged) edge** visible as the coin turns.


- Use a **metallic finish with a specular highlight that visibly rakes across the


  surface as it rotates**. A flat or matte disc is a fail.





## Below the hero





- An **About** section for the mint, with plausible on-brand copy.


- A **collection grid of exactly 4 coin editions**, each procedurally rendered in


  the same style — no placeholder boxes, no grey rectangles:


  1. **Bronze** — available


  2. **Silver** — available


  3. **Gold** — SOLD OUT: visually distinct and not purchasable


  4. **Black (obsidian)** — MINTING NEXT SEASON: name the season, not purchasable





## Required markup — grading depends on it





The hero canvas must be `<canvas id="coin-hero">`.





Each edition tile:





```html


<div class="edition" data-edition="bronze|silver|gold|black"


     data-state="available|sold-out|coming-soon">


  <canvas class="edition-canvas"></canvas>


  <button class="buy">Add to cart</button>   <!-- available editions ONLY -->


</div>


```





- Exactly 4 `.edition` tiles, one per `data-edition` value above.


- Available tiles carry an enabled `button.buy`. Sold-out and coming-soon tiles


  must have **no enabled `button.buy`**.


- The sold-out tile's text must contain "SOLD OUT". The coming-soon tile's text


  must name the season (spring / summer / autumn / fall / winter).





## Programmatic API — required for grading





```js


window.demo = {


  canvas,        // the hero <canvas> element


  setTime(t),    // t in [0,1] -> render EXACTLY that point of the loop,


                 // synchronously. Deterministic: the same t must always


                 // produce the same pixels.


  pause(),       // stop the animation loop (grading calls this first)


};


```





Grading pauses the animation, steps `setTime` across the loop, and analyses the


**rendered pixels**: the coin's silhouette through the turn, whether both faces


actually appear, and whether a specular highlight genuinely moves. Plausible


shader code that renders to a flat grey disc scores zero here — the render is


judged from output, never from source.





So the coin stays measurable: the hero canvas must contain **only the coin** on a


simple backdrop, and the coin must not touch the canvas edges.





Everything else — layout, type, palette, copy, motion design — is yours. This


ships to the real internet: CEOs, collectors, and the entire troll kingdom will


see it and criticise it. This is our only shot.

  // ---------- SHADER SOURCE ----------


  const vertexSrc = `#version 300 es


  in vec2 a_position;


  out vec2 v_uv;


  void main(){


    v_uv = a_position * 0.5 + 0.5;


    gl_Position = vec4(a_position,0.0,1.0);

  const fragmentSrc = `#version 300 es


  precision highp float;


  in vec2 v_uv;


  out vec4 outColor;


  uniform vec2 u_resolution;


  uniform float u_time;


  uniform vec3 u_color;





  // rotate a vec3 around Y axis


  mat3 rotY(float a){


    float c = cos(a), s = sin(a);


    return mat3(c,0.,s, 0.,1.,0., -s,0.,c);


  }


  // rotate around X axis


  mat3 rotX(float a){


    float c = cos(a), s = sin(a);


    return mat3(1.,0.,0., 0.,c,-s, 0.,s,c);


  }





  // Signed distance to a thin cylinder (coin) with ridged edge


  float sdCoin(vec3 p){


    // apply ridged edge via sinusoidal radius modulation


    float angle = atan(p.x, p.z);


    float ridges = 0.015 * sin(30.0*angle);


    float radius = 1.0 + ridges;


    float h = 0.07; // half thickness


    vec2 d = vec2(length(p.xz)-radius, abs(p.y)-h);


    return min(max(d.x,d.y),0.0) + length(max(d,0.0));


  }





  // normal by gradient


  vec3 calcNormal(vec3 p){


    float eps = 0.0005;


    vec2 e = vec2(1,-1)*eps;


    return normalize(e.xyy*sdCoin(p+e.xyy)+e.yyx*sdCoin(p+e.yyx)+e.yxy*sdCoin(p+e.yxy)+e.xxx*sdCoin(p+e.xxx));


  }





  // simple phong lighting with moving specular


  vec3 shade(vec3 p, vec3 rd){


    vec3 n = calcNormal(p);


    vec3 lightDir = normalize(vec3(0.8,0.6,0.7));


    vec3 viewDir = normalize(-rd);


    float diff = max(dot(n,lightDir),0.0);


    vec3 reflectDir = reflect(-lightDir,n);


    float spec = pow(max(dot(viewDir,reflectDir),0.0),64.0);


    vec3 col = u_color * (0.2 + 0.8*diff) + vec3(1.0)*spec;


    // rim lighting for extra metal feel


    float rim = pow(1.0 - max(dot(n,viewDir),0.0),2.0);


    col += vec3(0.2,0.2,0.3)*rim;


    return col;


  }





  void main(){


    vec2 uv = (v_uv*2.0-1.0) * vec2(u_resolution.x/u_resolution.y,1.0);


    // camera


    vec3 camPos = vec3(0.0,0.0,3.0);

    float t = u_time * 6.28318530718; // 0..2π


    mat3 rot = rotY(t) * rotX(t*0.7);


    // ray march


    float tDist = 0.0;


    float hit = -1.0;

  const w = canvas.offsetWidth;


  const h = canvas.offsetHeight;


  if (canvas.width !== w || canvas.height !== h) {


    canvas.width = w;


    canvas.height = h;


  }


}

    c.width = 256; c.height = 256;


    const ctx = c.getContext('2d');

        ctx.fill();

            ctx.rotate(angle);

            // Stylized blade shape


            ctx.moveTo(-50, -40); 


            ctx.lineTo(10, -20);


            ctx.lineTo(20, 60);


            ctx.lineTo(-50, 30);


            ctx.fill();


        }


        


        // Emboss simulation: Drop Shadow & Stroke


        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.8)';


        ctx.shadowBlur = 12;


        ctx.shadowOffsetX = 4;


        ctx.shadowOffsetY = 6;

function mat4Perspective(out,fovy,aspect,near,far){


  const f=1/Math.tan(fovy*0.5); const nf=1/(near-far);


  out[0]=f/aspect;out[1]=0;out[2]=0;out[3]=0;


  out[4]=0;out[5]=f;out[6]=0;out[7]=0;


  out[8]=0;out[9]=0;out[10]=(far+near)*nf;out[11]=-1;


  out[12]=0;out[13]=0;out[14]=2*far*near*nf;out[15]=0;


}


function compileShader(gl,type,src){const s=gl.createShader(type);gl.shaderSource(s,src);gl.compileShader(s);if(!gl.getShaderParameter(s,gl.COMPILE_STATUS))throw new Error(gl.getShaderInfoLog(s));return s;}


function createProgram(gl,vs,fs){const p=gl.createProgram();gl.attachShader(p,compileShader(gl,gl.VERTEX_SHADER,vs));gl.attachShader(p,compileShader(gl,gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER,fs));gl.linkProgram(p);if(!gl.getProgramParameter(p,gl.LINK_STATUS))throw new Error(gl.getProgramInfoLog(p));return p;}

vec3 rotateY(vec3 v,float a){float c=cos(a);float s=sin(a);return vec3(v.x*c + v.z*s, v.y, -v.x*s + v.z*c);}


void main(){


  vec3 pos=aPosition; pos=rotateX(pos,uRotX); pos=rotateY(pos,uRotY); pos.z-=4.0;

  vNormal=n; vUv=aUv; vFaceId=aFaceId;


  gl_Position=uProjectionMatrix*vec4(pos,1.0);


}`;

window.demo = {


  canvas: document.getElementById("coin-hero"),


  setTime: function(t) {


    let angle = t * 2 * Math.PI;


    const coinRadius = 50;


    const coinColor = '#ddd';


    const highlightColor = '#ccc';





    // Coin Shading (Simple, but functional)


    const ctx = this.canvas.getContext("2d");


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, this.canvas.width, this.canvas.height);





    ctx.save();


    ctx.translate(this.canvas.width / 2, this.canvas.height / 2);


    ctx.rotate(angle);





    // Front Face (WastedToken Mint Emblem) - Simplified for demonstration


    ctx.fillStyle = '#333';


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(0, 0, coinRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.fill();








    // Back Face (Token Count)


    ctx.rotate(Math.PI);


    ctx.fillStyle = '#666';


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(0, 0, coinRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.fill();





    // Reeded Edge - Simple ridge effect


     ctx.fillStyle = '#888';


     ctx.beginPath();


     ctx.moveTo(-coinRadius, 0);


     ctx.lineTo(coinRadius, 0);


     ctx.stroke();











    // Metallic Highlight (Specular Reflection)


    ctx.fillStyle = highlightColor;


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(0, 0, coinRadius * 0.8, angle, angle + Math.PI/6); // Adjusted for smoother rotation


    ctx.fill();








    ctx.restore();


  },


  pause: function() {


    this.canvas.style.cursor = 'default';


    document.getElementById("coin-hero").removeEventListener('click', null, true)


  }


};





let animationId;





function startAnimation() {


  animationId = requestAnimationFrame(function() {


    demo.setTime(0);


    startAnimation();


  });


}





demo.canvas.addEventListener('click', function() {


   demo.pause();


});








startAnimation();








</script>

const heroCanvas = document.getElementById('coin-hero');


const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({canvas: heroCanvas});


renderer.setSize(heroCanvas.clientWidth, heroCanvas.clientHeight);


const scene = new THREE.Scene();


const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera();


camera.position.z = 5;





const geometry = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.2, 1.2, 0.3, 64);


const material = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color: 0xaaaaaa, metalness: 1, roughness: 0});


const coin = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);


scene.add(coin);





const ambient = new THREE.AmbientLight();


scene.add(ambient);


const dirLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight();


dirLight.position.set(5, 5, 5);


scene.add(dirLight);





let isPaused = false;


function animate() {


  if (!isPaused) requestAnimationFrame(animate);


  const t = (performance.now()/2000)%1; // loop every 2 seconds


  setTime(t);


}


animate();

// Main coin setup for hero canvas


const mainCanvas = document.getElementById('coin-hero');


let renderer, scene, camera, coin;





function initHero() {


    const width = mainCanvas.clientWidth;


    const height = mainCanvas.clientHeight;


    


    renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ 


        canvas: mainCanvas, 


        antialias: true,


        alpha: false 


    });


    renderer.setSize(width, height);


    renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio);


    


    scene = new THREE.Scene();


    camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, width / height, 0.1, 1000);


    camera.position.z = 3;


    


    const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x888888);


    scene.add(ambientLight);


    


    const dirLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1);


    dirLight.position.set(5,3,2);


    scene.add(dirLight);





    // Create coin geometry and material


    const geometry = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1, 1, 0.1, 64);


    


    const emblemCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');


    emblemCanvas.width = 256;


    emblemCanvas.height = 256;


    const ctxE = emblemCanvas.getContext('2d');


    // Draw a star-like emblem with gradient for raised effect


    const grad = ctxE.createRadialGradient(128, 128, 0, 128, 128, 128);


    grad.addColorStop(0, 'white');


    grad.addColorStop(1, '#aaa');


    ctxE.fillStyle = grad;


    


    const centerX = 128, centerY = 128;


    for(let i=0; i<5; i++) {


        const angle = (i * Math.PI * 2) / 5 + Math.random() * -0.05;


        ctxE.beginPath();


        ctxE.moveTo(centerX, centerY);


        ctxE.lineTo(


            centerX + Math.cos(angle)*90,


            centerY + Math.sin(angle)*80


        );


    }


    ctxE.fill();





    const emblemTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(emblemCanvas);


    


    const frontMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: emblemTexture });


    


    // Back face with denomination


    const denomCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');


    denomCanvas.width = 256;


    denomCanvas.height = 256;


    const ctxD = denomCanvas.getContext('2d');


    ctxD.fillStyle='#fff';


    ctxD.font='30px sans-serif';


    ctxD.fillText('10,000', 40, 70);


    ctxD.fillText('TOKENS', 55, 120);


    const denomTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(denomCanvas);





    const backMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: denomTexture });





    // Side material with edge texture


    const sideCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');


    sideCanvas.width = 512;


    sideCanvas.height = 64;


    const ctxS = sideCanvas.getContext('2d');


    


    for(let y=0; y<64; y++) {


        if(y % 8 < 4) {


            ctxS.fillStyle='#ddd';


        } else {


            ctxS.fillStyle='#aaa';


        }


        ctxS.fillRect(0, y, sideCanvas.width, 1);


    }





    const edgeTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(sideCanvas);


    


    // Create a cylinder with front/back and sides materials


    coin = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, [frontMat, backMat]);


    


    scene.add(coin);


    


    animate(); // Start animation loop


}

    requestAnimationFrame(() => {

<script>


// WebGL Coin Renderer


const canvas = document.getElementById('coin-hero');


const gl = canvas.getContext('webgl');





let demo = {canvas};





function createTextureFromCanvas(gl, canvasElement) {


    const texture = gl.createTexture();


    gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, texture);


    gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, gl.RGBA, gl.RGBA, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, canvasElement);


    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);


    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);


    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, gl.LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR);


    return texture;


}

<!DOCTYPE html>


<html lang="en">


<head>


    <meta charset="UTF-8">


    <title>WastedToken Mint</title>


    <style>


        body {


            margin: 0;


            overflow: hidden;


            background-color: #000;


        }


        canvas {


            display: block;


        }


    </style>


    <script type="text/javascript">


        // WebGL setup


        var canvas = document.getElementById('coin-hero');


        var gl = window.demo.canvas;


        


        // Render timing


        function setTime(t) {


            gl.timestamp = t * 1000; // In milliseconds for timing loop


        }


        


        gl.pause();


        


    </script>


</head>


<body>


    <canvas id="coin-hero"></canvas>


    


    <script type="text/javascript">


        // Coin geometry (procedural, no external images)


        var coinVertices = [


            -500, 100, 0, 350, 250,


            150, 100, 200, 220, 120,


            50, 40, 80, 60, 10,


            -10, 15, 30, 25


        ];


        


        // Create mesh (procedural without external assets)


        function createMesh(vertices) {


            var v = new THREE.Vector3();


            vertices.forEach(function(v) {


                v.x = v.y;


                v.y = v.z;


                v.z = -v.x;


            });


            


            var position = new THREE.Matrix4().rotateZ(-Math.PI/2);


            var normal = new THREE.CoveredGeometry(position, 0.05).toGPUObject();


            return normal.getWorldPointAtPosition(vertices[0].position);


        }


        


        // Draw coin on canvas


        function drawCoin() {


            gl.clearColor(0x000, 0x123, 0x456, 1);


            gl.clearMatrix();


            


            var mesh = createMesh(coinVertices);


            gl.drawElements(THREE.POINTS, vertexCount, 3, THREE.VERTEX_ARRAY);


            


            // Apply rotation for 3D effect


            var centerX = 400, centerY = 200;


            var rotationMatrix = [


                1,0,0.5,


                -1,0,0,


                0,-0.707,0.414


            ];


            var matrix = new THREE.Matrix4().multiply(matrix);


            var cosTheta = Math.cos(Math.PI/2); // 90 degrees rotation


            


            // Rotate vertices around center point (400,200)


            coinVertices.forEach(function(v) {


                var rotatedX = v.x + cosTheta * (v.y - centerY) - sinTheta * (v.z - centerZ);


                var rotatedY = v.y + sinTheta * (v.x - centerX) + cosTheta * (v.z - centerZ);


            });


            


            // Translate to avoid edge touching canvas


            var translatedX = centerX + 10, translatedY = centerY + 5;


            


            gl.clearMatrix();


            gl.useMatrix(matrix);


            gl.translate(translatedX, translatedY);


            


            gl.drawMesh(new THREE.PointsMaterial({


                color: 0x00,


                vertexColors: [0x000],


                opacity: 1


            }, {size: 200, count: 8}));


        }


        


        // Run loop for 1000ms (enough time for one full coin rotation)


        setTime(50); // Set initial time before drawing


        gl.clearColor(0x00, 0x123, 0x456, 1.0);


        gl.clearMatrix();


        drawCoin();


    </script>


</body>


</html>

Claude has no cost figure here, on purpose. Every Claude model is measured through the Claude Code CLI, which runs on a subscription — there is no per-token price to report, so any number would be invented. Every other row is what the provider billed, or list pricing on real token counts. We tried publishing a derived API-equivalent and measurement refuted it twice: the CLI sends its own instructions, reads its own files and carries its own conversation, so it consumed 16× more input per turn than the same model doing the same task through an API — a different agent, not a fixed overhead, and nothing you can subtract. Claude cost lands here when the full suite has been run through the API, not before.
Latest-per-model table ranks by score, then cost, then speed. Token counts come from provider usage fields. Outputs are verbatim transcripts, truncated for display — full text lives in runs/…/transcript.jsonl.
